PICES Data Catalogue: ODIS JSON-LD directory

This directory holds one JSON-LD file per record of the PICES Data Catalogue (a community on Zenodo), expressed with schema.org following the ODIS book patterns. It is published by the North Pacific Marine Science Organization (PICES) so that the IOC Ocean Data and Information System (ODIS) can harvest a PICES-controlled description of the catalogue. Zenodo hosts the records and mints their DOIs.

  • Records: 3920 (2001 datasets, 1919 reports and other works)
  • Last refreshed from Zenodo: 2026-10-24T23:21:00+00:00
  • Sitemap for harvesters: sitemap.xml
  • Catalogue node: catalog.jsonld; organisation node: organization.jsonld
  • Base URL of this directory: https://pices-data.github.io/odis/
